<span class="icon icon-search"></span>
<input autocomplete="off" class="input-text" id="autocomplete-input-search" name="q" onclick="ga('send', 'event', 'Accès principaux', 'navigation', 'Rechercher');" placeholder="Que recherchez-vous ?" readonly="" type="text" value=""/>

<script>
                jQuery(document).on('ready', function() {

                    var serviceUrl = 'https://saintmaclou-prod.prediggo.io'; // Those fields have to be replaced with specific values
                    var apiKey = 'prod-stm-pub-QIO9YT98328E9S6E';
                    var sessionId = 'cb6rroue2o9h3fo0l62hvm7p97';

                    var options = {
                        /*customerId: 'customer-name-1234',*/
                        languageCode: 'fr',
                        zoneName: 'FR'
                    };

                    // The autocomplete is not linked to a page, hence, if no other componenent is present on the page, we can leave 'pageId' undefined or null.
                    var service = new PrediggoService(serviceUrl, apiKey, sessionId, undefined, options);

                    var templating = new Templating(service);

                    var dropdownTemplateId = '#dropdown-template';
                    var autocompleteParentId = '#autocomplete-parent';

                    var inputId = '.autocomplete-input';

                    var autoCompleteConfig = {
                        inputId: inputId,
                        autocompleteParentId: autocompleteParentId,
                        dropdownTemplateId: dropdownTemplateId,
                        onSuccess: function(data) {
                            console.log('Data: ' + JSON.stringify(data));
                            jQuery('#autocomplete-input-search').prop('disabled', true);
                            jQuery('#block-reco').hide();
                            jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').show();

                            if(jQuery('#autocomplete-parent .content-prediggo-1').find('.autocomplete-slots').length > 0) {
                                jQuery('.content-prediggo-1 .empty_result').hide();
                                jQuery('.see-all-products').show();
                                var productLabel = jQuery('#autocomplete-parent .content-prediggo-1 .autocomplete-slots-label span'),
                                    searchText = jQuery('.autocomplete-input ').val();

                                productLabel.each(function() {
                                    var regex = RegExp(searchText+'|'+searchText.toUpperCase(), 'gi'),
                                        replacement = '<strong>$&</strong>';

                                    jQuery(this).html(function() {
                                        return jQuery(this).text().replace(regex, replacement);
                                    });
                                });
                            } else {
                                jQuery('.content-prediggo-1 .empty_result').show();
                                jQuery('.see-all-products').hide();
                            }
                            if(jQuery('#autocomplete-parent .content-prediggo-2').find('.autocomplete-slots').length > 0) {
                                jQuery('.content-prediggo-2 .empty_result').hide();
                                var categLabel = jQuery('#autocomplete-parent .content-prediggo-2 .autcomplete-slots-label-cat span'),
                                    searchText = jQuery('.autocomplete-input ').val();

                                categLabel.each(function() {
                                    var regex = RegExp(searchText+'|'+searchText.toUpperCase(), 'gi'),
                                        replacement = '<strong>$&</strong>';

                                    jQuery(this).html(function() {
                                        return jQuery(this).text().replace(regex, replacement);
                                    });
                                });
                            } else {
                                jQuery('.content-prediggo-2 .empty_result').show();
                            }

                            var inputPurge = jQuery('.autocomplete-search').find('.picto_search');
                            inputPurge.on('click', function() {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-input').val('').attr('value','');
                                jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').hide();
                                jQuery('#block-reco').show();
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-input').focus();
                            });

                            jQuery('.autocomplete-container .autocomplete-input').keyup(function() {
                                if(jQuery(this).val().length < 3) {
                                    jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').hide();
                                    jQuery('#block-reco').show();
                                }
                                else {
                                    jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').show();
                                    jQuery('#block-reco').hide();
                                }
                            });

                        },
                        onError: function(data) { console.log('Err: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); }
                    };

                    var autocomplete = new AutocompleteWidget(autoCompleteConfig, templating);

                    jQuery.fn.closeAutocompleteHeader = function () {
                        jQuery(this).on('click', function() {
                            jQuery('#autocomplete-input-search').prop('disabled', false);
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-container').hide();
                            jQuery('#block-reco').empty();
                            jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').empty();
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').hide();
                            jQuery(".autocomplete-form").removeClass('active');
                            jQuery("#header #prediggo-search #autocomplete-input").val('');
                            if(jQuery(document).width() <= 979) {
                                jQuery("#header #prediggo-search #autocomplete-input-search").hide();
                                jQuery("#header #prediggo-search .autocomplete-close").hide();
                            }
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-input').focus();
                        })
                    };

                    jQuery(document).on('click','#autocomplete-input-search', function() {
                        jQuery('.autocomplete-input').val('');
                        if(jQuery(document).width() >= 979) {
                            if (jQuery('#promo-banner').length) {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-container').css('top', '58px');
                            } else {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-container').css('top', '20px');
                            }
                        }
                        if(jQuery(this).val() == '') {
                            var config = {

                                blockId: 0,

                                blockParentId: '#block-reco',
                                blockTemplateId: '#reco-template',

                                onSuccess: function(data) {
                                    console.log('Data: ' + JSON.stringify(data));
                                    jQuery('#autocomplete-input-search').prop('disabled', true);
                                    jQuery('#block-reco').show();
                                    jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').hide()
                                    jQuery('.autocomplete-container').show();
                                    jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').show();
                                    jQuery('.autocomplete-input').focus();

                                    var inputPurge = jQuery('.autocomplete-search').find('.picto_search');
                                    inputPurge.on('click', function() {
                                        jQuery('#block-reco').show();
                                        jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').hide();
                                        jQuery('.autocomplete-input').focus();
                                    });
                                },
                                onError: function(data) { console.log('Err: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); }

                            };
                            var recoWidget = new RecoWidget(config, templating);
                            recoWidget.fetchForGeneric();
                        }
                    });

                    jQuery(".autocomplete-overlay").closeAutocompleteHeader();

                    jQuery('.autocomplete-form .icon-search').on('click', function() {
                        if(jQuery(document).width() <= 979) {
                            if (jQuery('#promo-banner').length) {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-container').css('top', '0');
                            } else {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-container').css('top', '0');
                            }
                        }
                        if(jQuery(document).width() <= 979) {
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').toggle();
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-container').toggle();
                            jQuery(".autocomplete-form").toggleClass('active');
                            //jQuery("#header #prediggo-search .autocomplete-close").toggle();

                            if(jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').css('display') == 'block') {
                                jQuery('.autocomplete-input').val('');
                                jQuery(".autocomplete-input").focus();
                                var config = {

                                    blockId: 0,

                                    blockParentId: '#block-reco',
                                    blockTemplateId: '#reco-template',

                                    onSuccess: function(data) {
                                        if(jQuery('.autocomplete-input').val() == '') {
                                            jQuery('#block-reco').show();
                                            jQuery('#autocomplete-parent').hide();
                                        } else {
                                            jQuery('#block-reco').hide();
                                        }
                                        jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').show();
                                    },
                                    onError: function(data) { console.log('Err: ' + JSON.stringify(data)); }

                                };
                                var recoWidget = new RecoWidget(config, templating);
                                recoWidget.fetchForGeneric();
                            } else {
                                jQuery('#block-reco').hide();
                            }
                        }
                    });

                    jQuery(".autocomplete-close").closeAutocompleteHeader();

                    jQuery(".picto-search").closeAutocompleteHeader();

                    /*jQuery(autocompleteParentId+" "+inputId).on('click', function() {
                        var autocomplete = new AutocompleteWidget(autoCompleteConfig, templating);
                    });*/

                    templating.func.closeAutocomplete = function(element, json) {
                        element.find('.autocomplete-close').click(function(event) {
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-container').hide();
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-container').empty();
                            jQuery(".autocomplete-form").removeClass('active');
                            jQuery('.autocomplete-overlay').hide();
                            jQuery('#autocomplete-input-search').prop('disabled', false);
                            if(jQuery(document).width() <= 1200) {
                                jQuery("#header #prediggo-search #autocomplete-input-search").hide();
                                jQuery("#header #prediggo-search .autocomplete-close").hide();
                            }
                        });
                    };

                    templating.func.displayPrice = function(element, json) {
                        if(json.attributes.product_type.values[0].label=='Tapis')
                            element.find('.apartirde').show();
                        else
                            element.find('.apartirde').hide();

                        if ('m2' == element.find('.unite').attr("unity-parameter"))
                            element.find('.unite').html("le m2");
                        else if ('rouleau' == element.find('.unite').attr("unity-parameter"))
                            element.find('.unite').html("le rouleau");
                        else if ('metre' == element.find('.unite').attr("unity-parameter"))
                            element.find('.unite').html("le mètre linéaire");
                        else if ('piece' == element.find('.unite').attr("unity-parameter"))
                            element.find('.unite').html("pièce");

                        if(element.find('.promo').attr("decotte-parameter"))
                            element.find('.promo').show();
                        else
                            element.find('.promo').hide();
                        if(element.find('.prix-standard').attr("price-standard"))
                            element.find('.prix-standard').show();
                        else
                            element.find('.prix-standard').hide();

                    };

                    templating.func.displayCat = function(element, json) {
                        var id_cat=element.find('a').attr('id-cat-parameter');
                        var link_cat = id_cat.split(';;');
                        element.find('a').attr("href",link_cat[1]);
                    };


                    templating.func.displayBtnListProduct = function(element, json) {
                        var product_link="/pre/search_facetedSearch/search/?q="+element.find('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');
                        var nb_products = element.find('.see-all-products').attr('nb-result-parameter');
                        if(nb_products == '')
                            nb_products = 0;
                        var product_link_txt="> Voir tous les produits <span>("+nb_products+")</span>";

                        element.find('.see-all-products-link').attr("href",product_link);
                        element.find('.see-all-products-link').html(product_link_txt);
                    };

                    // Tracking GA
                    jQuery(document).on('click', '.content-prediggo-1 .autocomplete-slots a', function() {
                        var productParent = jQuery(this).parent('div');
                        var label = productParent.attr('gaLabel');
                        var ref, query, nbResults = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');

                        if(label === "Suggestions") {
                            query = jQuery(this).children('span').html();
                            ga('send', 'event', 'recherche interne', label, query, nbResults);
                        }
                        else {
                            ref = productParent.attr('gaRef');
                            query = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');
                            ga('send', 'event', 'recherche interne', 'produits - '+ref+' - '+label, query, nbResults);
                        }

                    });

                    jQuery(document).on('click', '.content-prediggo-2 .autocomplete-slots a', function() {
                        var productParent = jQuery(this).parent('div');
                        var label = jQuery(this).text();
                        var ref = jQuery(this).attr('id-cat-parameter');
                        var link_cat = ref.split(';;');
                        var nbResults = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('nb-result-parameter');
                        var query = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');
                        ga('send', 'event', 'recherche interne', 'Catégories - '+link_cat[0]+' - '+label, query, nbResults);
                    });

                    jQuery(document).on('click', '.content-prediggo-3 .autocomplete-item-moment', function() {
                        var url = jQuery(this).attr('href');
                        var nbResults = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('nb-result-parameter');
                        var query = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');
                        ga('send', 'event', 'recherche interne', 'En ce moment - '+url, query, nbResults);
                    });

                    jQuery(document).on('click', '.content-prediggo-3 .autocomplete-item-conseil', function() {
                        var label = jQuery(this).text();
                        var nbResults = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('nb-result-parameter');
                        var query = jQuery('.see-all-products').attr('query-parameter');
                        ga('send', 'event', 'recherche interne', 'Conseils - '+label, query, nbResults);
                    });
                });
            </script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    jQuery(document).ready(function() {
        /* SELECT PROJET */
        jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-project-detail-block').hide();

        /* BTN RDV */
        jQuery('#contact-address').hide();
        jQuery('#contact-address-complement').hide();
        jQuery('#contact-postal-code').hide();
        jQuery('#contact-city').hide();

        jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-contact-type-list li').click(function () {
            var btnPopin = jQuery(this).attr('data-display-form');

            if (btnPopin==='phone' || btnPopin==='shop')
            {
                /* Initialisation */
                jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-contact-type-list li').removeClass('active');
                jQuery('.icon-house-orange').hide();
                jQuery('.icon-house').show();
                jQuery('.icon-phone-orange').hide();
                jQuery('.icon-phone').show();
                jQuery('.icon-shop-orange').hide();
                jQuery('.icon-shop').show();
                /* Fin Initialisation */

                jQuery(this).addClass('active');
                jQuery(this).removeClass('smc-form-error');
                jQuery('.icon-'+btnPopin).hide();
                jQuery('.icon-'+btnPopin+'-orange').show();

                jQuery('#contact-address').hide();
                jQuery('#contact-address-complement').hide();
                jQuery('#contact-postal-code').hide();
                jQuery('#contact-city').hide();

                jQuery('#contact-address').prop('required', false);
                jQuery('#contact-postal-code').prop('required', false);
                jQuery('#contact-city').prop('required', false);

                jQuery("input[name=contact_mode]").val(btnPopin);
            }
            else if (btnPopin==='house')
            {
                /* Initialisation */
                jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-contact-type-list li').removeClass('active');
                jQuery('.icon-phone-orange').hide();
                jQuery('.icon-phone').show();
                jQuery('.icon-shop-orange').hide();
                jQuery('.icon-shop').show();
                /* Fin Initialisation */

                jQuery(this).addClass('active');
                jQuery(this).removeClass('smc-form-error');
                jQuery('.icon-'+btnPopin).hide();
                jQuery('.icon-'+btnPopin+'-orange').show();

                jQuery('#contact-address').show();
                jQuery('#contact-address-complement').show();
                jQuery('#contact-postal-code').show();
                jQuery('#contact-city').show();

                jQuery('#contact-address').prop('required', true);
                jQuery('#contact-postal-code').prop('required', true);
                jQuery('#contact-city').prop('required', true);

                jQuery("input[name=contact_mode]").val('home');
            }
        });

        jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-project-detail-block').hide();
        jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-project-type-select').change(function () {
            if(jQuery(this).val()==='floor') {
                jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-project-detail-block').show();
            }
            else {
                jQuery('.popin-fullscreen-devis #estimate-project-detail-block').hide();
            }
        });
    });
</script>
</div>
